class="text-sm leading-5"><div class="overflow-hidden line-clamp-3">The Core Consumer Price Index (CPI) measures the changes in the price of goods
and services, excluding food and energy. The CPI measures price change from the perspective of the consumer. It is a key way to measure changes in purchasing trends and <a href="https://www.investing.com/academy/trading/inflation-definition/">inflation</a>.<br/><br/>A higher than expected reading should be taken as positive/bullish for the USD, while a lower than expected reading should be taken as negative/bearish for the USD.</div><div class="mt-2"><button class="flex items-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap text-sm font-semibold text-link hover:underline">Show more<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" class="h-4 w-4"><use
